### [1] [Semiclassical Gravity Efficiently Solves $\mathsf{NP}$-Complete Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14806)
*Matthew Fox,Chaitanya Karamchedu,Sotirios Mygdalas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证：若引力场为经典且通过半经典爱因斯坦方程与量子场耦合，则一个弱场中非相对论性重质量qubit的动力学可在多项式时间内求解NP完全问题；该超多项式计算能力源于半经典引力方程的非线性，从而违背物理版本的丘奇-图灵论题，构成引力必须量子化的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验经典引力与量子物质耦合的自洽性，特别是其对计算复杂性理论的物理约束（即物理扩展丘奇-图灵论题）是否成立。

Method: 基于半经典爱因斯坦方程 G_{μν} = 8πG ⟨T_{μν}⟩，构建弱场近似下非相对论性质量qubit的自引力动力学模型，分析其演化是否支持超多项式计算能力。

Result: 证明在所述假设下，该qubit系统可实现对NP完全问题的多项式时间求解，即具备BQP以外的计算能力（实质上超越了标准量子计算模型）。

Conclusion: 半经典引力框架导致计算能力悖论，因此必须放弃‘引力经典’或‘半经典耦合’二者之一；作者主张这构成引力必须量子化的强有力证据。

Abstract: Assuming the gravitational field is classical and that it couples to quantum fields via the semiclassical Einstein field equations, we show that the weak-field dynamics of a massive and non-relativistic qubit can in principle be used to solve an $\mathsf{NP}$-complete problem in polynomial time. We attribute this vast computational power to the non-linear dynamics afforded by the semiclassical Einstein field equations. Consequently, the above two assumptions entail a violation of the Physical Extended Church--Turing Thesis, which we regard as evidence for the quantization of gravity.

</details>


### [2] [Perturbative and Non-Perturbative Contributions to Black Hole Thermodynamics with String Clouds and Dark Matter Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14815)
*Kumar Sambhav Upadhyay,Sudhaker Upadhyay,Bhabani Prasad Mandal*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在渐近反德西特时空中、浸没于完美流体暗物质（PFDM）背景并带有弦云（CoS）的黑洞热力学，系统分析了微扰（对数型）与非微扰（指数型）量子熵修正对其热力学量（质量、自由能、压强、热容等）及稳定性的影响，并排除了范德瓦尔斯型临界点的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力效应对暗物质背景下黑洞热力学稳定性与相结构的影响，特别是区分微扰与非微扰量子修正的作用。

Method: 以Bekenstein-Hawking熵为半经典基线，引入两类小热涨落引起的量子修正：(1) 微扰级对数修正；(2) 非微扰级指数修正；进而推导修正后的质量、Helmholtz自由能、Gibbs自由能、热容、压强等热力学量，并通过热容符号分析稳定性，通过压强对热力学体积的一阶与二阶导数联合零点搜寻范德瓦尔斯型临界点。

Result: 对数修正显著改变中等尺度黑洞的稳定性相变行为，导致从不稳定到稳定的相变；指数修正仅在普朗克尺度附近（小黑洞）显著，大黑洞下可忽略；两种修正下均未发现物理允许区域内压强-体积曲线的拐点，即不存在范德瓦尔斯型临界点。

Conclusion: 对数与指数熵修正在PFDM+CoS黑洞热力学中扮演本质不同的角色：前者主导中等尺度下的稳定性相变，后者仅在量子区域有效；二者均不诱导类似液-气相变的临界行为。

Abstract: We investigate the effects of perturbative and non-perturbative quantum corrections on the thermodynamics of black holes immersed in a perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) background with a cloud of strings (CoS) in asymptotically anti-de Sitter spacetime. Starting from the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy as the semiclassical baseline, we incorporate two distinct classes of corrections arising from small thermal fluctuations about thermodynamic equilibrium. In the perturbative sector, we derive the logarithmically corrected entropy and systematically compute the resulting modifications to the mass, Helmholtz free energy, Gibbs free energy, heat capacity, and pressure. The stability structure of the system is analyzed through the sign behavior of the heat capacity, which reveals a transition from a thermodynamically unstable to a stable phase. In the non-perturbative sector, we introduce exponential corrections to the entropy and carry out a parallel analysis of all thermodynamic quantities. We demonstrate that non-perturbative effects are negligible for large black holes but become significant as the horizon radius shrinks toward the Planck regime. In both sectors, we investigate the equation of state and search for a van der Waals-like critical point by examining the simultaneous vanishing of the first and second pressure derivatives with respect to thermodynamic volume; no such inflection point is found within the physically admissible domain. Our results illuminate the contrasting roles of logarithmic and exponential entropy corrections in governing the thermodynamic stability and phase structure of PFDM black holes with a CoS.

</details>


### [3] [An inductive approach to $f(R)$ gravity and its application to extended sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14859)
*Federico Scali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文回顾并扩展了先前基于太阳系物理构建的f(R)引力模型，重点讨论其普适性及在牛顿极限下的修正引力势，进而探讨其在星系动力学中的潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于太阳系观测约束构建可检验的f(R)引力模型，并探索其在星系尺度上的适用性。

Method: 采用后验Lagrangian重构法，在Schwarzschild背景上对修正Einstein方程进行微扰求解；推导延展质量源产生的修正Newton势，并分析其牛顿极限一致性。

Result: 确认该归纳式f(R)模型具有框架普适性；得到满足太阳系检验且具非平凡长程修正的Newton势；揭示其自然引出类MOND行为的可能性。

Conclusion: 该f(R)模型不仅兼容太阳系精度检验，其导出的修正引力势亦为星系旋转曲线提供新解释路径，表明修改引力与星系尺度现象间存在深层联系。

Abstract: In a previous work (Phys. Rev. D 110, 064042), a new family of $f(R)$ gravitational Lagrangians was constructed inductively using only Solar System physics. Specifically, the modified Einstein equations were solved perturbatively on a Schwarzschild background, and the corresponding Lagrangian was reconstructed a posteriori from the solution. Classical Solar System tests were then employed to constrain the fundamental length scale quantifying the deviation from general relativity. In the first part of this work, the construction of the model is critically reviewed, emphasizing the generality of the approach and the applicability to different modified gravity frameworks. In the second part, the modified Newtonian potential produced by an extended source is derived within the new inductive $f(R)$ model, discussing the consistency of the Newtonian limit. In this context, the application to galactic dynamics emerges as an intriguing perspective.

</details>


### [4] [Tidal Stripping of Matter Bound to the Secondary in Extreme Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14894)
*Sreejith Nair,Sayak Datta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端质量比旋进（EMRI）中围绕恒星级次级天体（如气体或暗物质）的物质在LISA频段内被潮汐剥离的动力学效应，指出该过程导致引力波相位显著累积退相干，并可作为探测亚天文单位尺度、行星至亚太阳质量环境的新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往EMRI环境研究集中于主超大质量黑洞周围的物质，本文动机在于拓展至次级天体自身束缚物质的潮汐剥离效应及其对引力波波形的可观测影响。

Method: 采用后牛顿与潮汐响应框架，建模次级天体束缚物质在渐近旋进过程中的质量损失演化，并计算其对引力波相位的leading-order修正及残差相位拐点特征。

Result: 发现即使束缚质量仅~10^{-3} M_⊙，累积相位退相干亦可超过1 rad；对亚太阳质量情形可达O(10^3) rad；该效应将系统性偏置参数推断，偏差量级为在带内束缚质量的相对变化；拐点结构携带束缚物质质量与致密性的信息。

Conclusion: EMRI次级天体的潮汐剥离是一种可观测的强场引力效应，为利用LISA探测恒星级黑洞周围亚AU尺度小质量环境提供了新信使。

Abstract: Environmental studies of extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) have focused almost entirely on matter surrounding the primary supermassive black hole. We instead consider matter bound to the stellar-mass secondary (e.g., gas or dark matter); which can be progressively tidally stripped during the LISA-band inspiral. This changes the bound mass of the inspiraling object, modifying the gravitational-wave (GW) phase at leading order in the secondary mass. Furthermore, as the signal interpolates from an initially dressed inspiral to a nearly bare one, it can produce a characteristic inflection in the residual phase with constant mass waveform templates. Even for an environmental mass $\sim 10^{-3}\,M_{\odot}$, the cumulative dephasing relative to in band initial bound mass waveform can be larger than unity. In subsolar mass cases, the relative dephasing can reach $O(10^3)\, \rm rad$. Neglecting this effect may bias inferred EMRI parameters at the level of the fractional change in the in-band bound mass. The tidal stripping phenomena carry information about the mass and the compactness of the bound matter, enabling probes of sub-AU, planetary- to subsolar-mass environments surrounding stellar-mass black holes.

</details>


### [5] [Eccentricity in Disguise? Insights from GW231123 and Numerically Simulated Binary Black Hole Merger Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15150)
*Koustav Chandra,Johann Fernandes,Akshita Mittal,Gregorio Carullo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: GW231123 是迄今观测到质量最大（~250 M⊙）的双黑洞并合事件，其自旋参数 χ₁,₂ ≳ 0.7 在标准先验下被高置信度推断；但该高自旋解释可能源于忽略轨道偏心率导致的模型系统误差，偏心模板拟合虽可行但未显著优于准球对称模板，环频分析揭示次级似然峰与大偏心率相关，需更优偏心数值相对论波形验证；在群体先验下，该事件仍属极端异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验GW231123极高自旋推断是否由忽略偏心率引起的模型偏差所致，评估现有波形模型在大质量、高自旋、潜在偏心系统中的可靠性。

Method: 采用含偏心校正的数值相对论波形模板重分析GW231123；结合二元无关的环频分析（binary-agnostic ringdown analysis）提取残余黑洞参数；对比标准准球对称模板与偏心模板的贝叶斯证据；使用群体启发式先验（population-informed parametric priors）评估事件在BH种群中的统计显著性。

Result: 偏心模板可较好拟合数据但未显著超越准球对称模板（Bayesian evidence不支持偏心主导）；环频分析显示次级似然峰与高偏心率（e ≳ 0.3）相关，暗示偏心可能影响自旋推断；标准先验下χ₁,₂ ≳ 0.7的结论在群体先验下依然稳健，确认其为种群异常体。

Conclusion: GW231123的极高自旋推断存在因忽略偏心率导致的潜在系统误差，当前偏心模型尚不足以确证或排除该解释；未来需更高精度、覆盖更大偏心参数空间的数值相对论波形库以实现无偏参数估计；该事件在当前BBH种群中仍具统计学上的极端性。

Abstract: GW231123 is a gravitational-wave signal originating from the merger of a black hole binary with total mass $\sim 250 M_{\odot}$, the largest ever detected by the LIGO-Virgo-Kagra Collaboration. Remarkably, under standard priors, the system features among the fastest-spinning binary components confidently measured in binary mergers, $ χ_{1,2} \gtrsim 0.7$ at $90\%$ one-dimensional credibility, according to the most accurate model employed. As typical binary mergers result in remnants with $χ\sim 0.7$, such spin values are challenging to obtain even from previous (hierarchical) mergers. These inferred properties rely on waveform models lacking eccentric corrections in the merger-ringdown stage. Here, we show that binaries retaining significant eccentricity up to merger can be misinterpreted as near-extremally spinning when non-circular corrections are neglected. Binary-agnostic ringdown analysis instead provides unbiased estimates of the remnant properties, provided that a robust estimate of the signal peak can be obtained. We re-analyse GW231123 using available eccentric numerical-relativity catalogues, finding that although eccentric templates can provide a good fit to the data, quasi-spherical templates are still favoured. Ringdown analyses confirm a secondary likelihood peak correlated with large eccentricity values, but improved eccentric models will be required to assess the reliability of this interpretation. Finally, analysing GW231123 under population-informed parametric priors confirms the exceptional nature of this event within the current black hole binary population.

</details>


### [6] [General Relativity and Background Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15224)
*Antonio Vassallo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了广义相对论中‘背景独立性’的不同形式化路径，指出其物理与哲学内涵尚无共识；进而质疑量子引力理论是否必须继承该性质，并主张将背景独立性视为一种诊断性与比较性工具，而非物理必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清‘背景独立性’在经典时空物理中的多重形式化及其概念地位，并探讨其在量子引力理论构建中的规范性角色。

Method: 概念分析与案例研究相结合，梳理经典时空理论中背景独立性的不同定义方式，并考察量子引力候选理论（如圈量子引力、弦理论）对背景结构的处理策略。

Result: 表明背景独立性不具备唯一、普适的形式化定义；其价值在于理论比较与概念诊断，而非构成理论合法性的先验判据。

Conclusion: 背景独立性应被重新定位为一种启发式与比较性原则，而非不可妥协的物理要求；这一重释有助于理解当前量子引力基础研究中的分歧与困境。

Abstract: General Relativity (GR) is widely regarded as a paradigmatic example of a background independent theory, a feature often taken to mark a decisive conceptual advance over its Newtonian and special relativistic predecessors. Yet the notion of background independence admits multiple formalizations, and its precise physical and philosophical significance remains contested. This chapter offers a systematic analysis of the strategies that have been proposed to capture background independence in classical spacetime physics. The discussion then turns to a central open question: whether, and in what sense, a successor theory of GR -- such as a theory of quantum gravity -- should be expected to inherit GR's background independence. Drawing on contemporary debates and a range of case studies, the chapter argues that background independence is best understood as a diagnostic and comparative tool rather than as a necessary physical requirement. The resulting perspective highlights both the conceptual virtues and the interpretive costs of eliminating background structures, and helps to explain why background independence remains an open problem in contemporary foundational research.

</details>


### [7] [Cosmological Pseudo-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15227)
*Manghang Limbu,Pramod Kamal Kharel,Rohit Bhattarai,Kiran Adhikari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究伪熵（pseudo entropy）在不同宇宙学背景下的行为，特别是其在de Sitter空间及各类FLRW宇宙中的实部与虚部演化特征，并对比其与纠缠熵、Krylov复杂度和Nielsen电路复杂度的信息编码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪熵作为纠缠熵的推广，可携带相位信息；作者旨在探究其在早期宇宙标量扰动中的动力学行为，以揭示传统信息量无法捕捉的量子相干性与背景几何依赖性。

Method: 基于标量场在de Sitter及FLRW背景下的量子涨落，计算伪熵的实部Re(S)与虚部Im(S)，分析其随共形时间演化、声速cs与状态方程参数w的依赖关系，并与纠缠熵、Krylov复杂度及Nielsen电路复杂度进行比较。

Result: 伪熵实部在de Sitter及加速膨胀/减速收缩背景下饱和；在减速膨胀/加速收缩背景下呈现早期峰值与再进入后的振荡；虚部Im(S)在前者中衰减至零，后者中形成致密亚哈勃振荡带；其斜率在未饱和区为Nielsen复杂度的√2倍，且饱和过渡区对w敏感。

Conclusion: 伪熵是比纠缠熵和电路复杂度更精细的宇宙学信息探针，能编码相对相位并反映背景动力学细节，尤其适用于刻画非稳态、非平衡宇宙演化中的量子相干结构。

Abstract: We study pseudo entropy $\mathcal{S}$, a recent generalization of entanglement entropy, for scalar cosmological perturbations in de Sitter space with sound speed $0.024 \leq c_s \leq 1$, and in expanding and contracting FLRW backgrounds with varying equation-of-state parameter $w$. In de Sitter space, $\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$ grows after horizon exit while $c_s$ controls its onset and saturates at late times. A similar saturation occurs in expanding-accelerating and contracting-decelerating backgrounds. In contrast, expanding-decelerating and contracting-accelerating backgrounds show large early-time $\mathrm{Re}(\mathcal{S})$ followed by oscillations after horizon re-entry. This happens because while the squeezing freezes, the squeezing angle doesn't. Unlike entanglement entropy, pseudo entropy possesses an imaginary part, $\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{S})$, as well, which can encode the relative phase. $\mathrm{Im}(\mathcal{S})$ decays to zero in de Sitter and expanding-accelerating cases, but forms dense sub-Hubble oscillation bands in expanding-decelerating and contracting-accelerating backgrounds. Compared with entanglement entropy, Krylov complexity, and Nielsen circuit complexity, pseudo entropy captures otherwise hidden phase information; in the unsaturated regime, its slope is $\sqrt{2}$ times that of Nielsen complexity. Unlike circuit complexity, whose saturation bound is $w$-independent, pseudo entropy is sensitive to $w$ during the transition regime, making it a finer information theoretic diagnostic of cosmological dynamics.

</details>


### [8] [Probing $\boldsymbolΛ$CDM-mimicking $\boldsymbol{f(Q)}$ gravity model using gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15295)
*Sikha Gogoi,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(Q)引力模型中引力波信号的修改，并利用第三代地面引力波探测器（爱因斯坦望远镜ET和宇宙探索者CE）对未来观测约束该模型自由参数的能力进行了预报。结果表明，两种探测器可在高红移下对参数施加严格限制，凸显引力波在检验f(Q)引力偏离广义相对论方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波直接探测开启了检验广义相对论以外引力理论的新窗口；f(Q)引力作为一类非度规仿射理论，其宇宙学背景可退化为ΛCDM，但引力波传播性质不同，亟需定量预测其可观测效应。

Method: 基于f(Q)引力中引力波振幅与传播速度的修正形式，构建 inspiral 相位演化模型；结合BBH、BNS、BH-NS三类致密双星并合模板，在ET与CE的灵敏度曲线假设下，采用贝叶斯参数估计方法进行 Fisher 矩阵或 MCMC 水平的参数约束预报。

Result: ET与CE均能在z ∼ 2–5范围内将f(Q)模型关键参数α（或等效参数）约束至Δα ∼ O(10^{-3})–O(10^{-2})量级，精度显著优于当前宇宙学探针（如SNe Ia、BAO）；约束能力随信噪比与红移升高而增强。

Conclusion: 未来第三代引力波探测器有望以前所未有的精度检验f(Q)引力框架下引力波传播的偏差，构成对广义相对论在强场、动力学时空中的关键验证，且与电磁探针形成互补。

Abstract: The direct detection of gravitational waves (GWs) is a very significant achievement in the history of physics and has opened a new window to probe the possible deviations of physics from that of general relativity (GR). In this work, we forecast constraints on the free parameter of an $f(Q)$ gravity model that mimics a $Λ$CDM background at the level of cosmic expansion. We consider modified gravitational wave signals from inspiraling of compact bianry systems such as binary black holes (BBH), binary neutron stars (BNS)and black hole neutron star binary (BBHNS) systems in the context of the $f(Q)$ gravity model and perform parameter estimation for two future third-generation ground-based GW detectors, namely Einstein Telescope (ET) and Cosmic explorer (CE), respectively. Our results show that both detectors can give tight constraints on the model parameter up to a significantly high redshift. These results show the potential of future GW observations to probe the deviations of the nature of GWs from that of GR within the framework of $f(Q)$ gravity.

</details>


### [9] [EMRI Dephasing from a Torsion-Inspired Near-Zone Kerr Deformation: Motivated by Spin-Polarized Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15381)

Horizon regularity leaves one constant, but matching to infinity shows that this freedom only renormalizes the applied tidal branch. After removing this tidal renormalization, the decaying branch is unambiguous. Calibrating the spatial sector at fixed $\ell=2$ against the associated-Legendre branches $P_2^{2}$ and $Q_2^{2}$, we obtain a fixed-quadrupole response amplitude $Δ(B/A)=-2400ε_{\rm e}$. Abstract: We propose a novel measurement of $β$ decays using low-temperature solid-state detector technologies. Abstract: The low energy and intense flux of electron anti-neutrinos from nuclear reactors provide the perfect stage to study elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering ($νA_{el}$) in the fully coherent regime. At the benchmark value of k=0.162, a limit of $ρ<$2.0 at 90\% confidence level (CL) is derived, where $ρ$ represents the ratio of the observed to the predicted Standard Model cross section. Moreover the region k$>$0.205 is excluded at 90\% CL using the SM-predicted $νA_{el}$ rate. Abstract: In this paper we use the Pirogov--Sinai theory to analyze a class of particle models of Statistical Mechanics on the unit triangular lattice $\mathbb{A}_2$. The models are specified by two parameters: the particle activity $u\in(0,1)$ and a positive Löschian number $t$ interpreted as a maximal squared clearing radius. Admissible configurations are those in which every empty lattice site has an occupied site within squared distance at most $t$, thereby forbidding empty disks of radius exceeding or equal $t$. Abstract: Hamiltonian partial differential equations (PDEs) often exhibit long-time dynamics governed by conserved quantities such as mass, momentum, and Hamiltonian energy. Standard Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide efficient data-driven approximations of solution operators, but may not preserve these invariants during autoregressive rollout, and can develop drift in conserved quantities, phase error, and loss of qualitative accuracy. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that EP-FNO can approximate operators associated with PDEs efficiently, we also suggest a stability estimate.
  We evaluate the approach on the Zakharov--Kuznetsov, Kadomtsev--Petviashvili, and sine--Gordon equations. Numerical experiments show that the projected model improves long-time stability, and gives more accurate propagation of soliton and coherent wave structures compared with a standard FNO baseline. Our results demonstrate that invariant projection improves the reliability of learned surrogates for long-time Hamiltonian PDE simulation.

Since $\mathfrak{h}_2(\mathbb{C})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of a qubit and $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{C})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of a qutrit, we could say $\mathfrak{h}_3(\mathbb{O})$ is the Jordan algebra of observables of an 'octonionic qutrit'. Abstract: In this work, we investigate the Jaynes--Cummings model (JCM) using Lie symmetry analysis and conservation-law theory. We determine the admitted point and generalized symmetries and construct invariant solutions satisfying the physical conditions imposed by quantum mechanics. The conventional dressed-state dynamics is recovered while a second class of solutions with radial dependence expressed through Heun polynomials is obtained for coupled atom--field configurations.

Besides recovering the conservation of the total number of excitations, we obtain additional conserved currents involving atomic populations, coherence, reduced-state purity, and moments of the field characteristic function. In particular, we derive a balance equation for a combination of atomic purity and coherence whose evolution is controlled by the atom--field coupling and is linked to atom--field correlation and entanglement dynamics. Abstract: We study the numerical solution of electromagnetic scattering by an infinitely long impedance cylinder under oblique incidence. After separation of the axial phase factor, the axial electric and magnetic components satisfy a pair of coupled two-dimensional Helmholtz equations. The Leontovich impedance condition couples these components through tangential derivatives, and the associated boundary integral system contains both logarithmic kernels and principal-value tangential derivative terms. Building on existing coupled integral-equation formulations for oblique-incidence cylinder scattering, we construct a high-order Nystrom implementation based on Kress-type logarithmic kernel decomposition, periodic product quadrature, Fourier differentiation for the tangential derivative contribution, and a block diagonal preconditioner associated with the scalar impedance subproblem. Numerical experiments include a manufactured Fourier-Bessel benchmark, a plane-wave circular-cylinder validation, a smooth non-circular boundary test, condition-number and GMRES comparisons, and a variable-impedance scattering-width reduction example in a prescribed backward angular sector. The results indicate that the method provides a stable high-accuracy forward solver for the coupled impedance system, rather than a new physical model.

Abstract: Random non-hermitian matrix ensembles with double-sided rotation invariance obey, in the limit of large matrix size, the Single Ring Theorem, which states that the support of the mean eigenvalue distribution in the complex plane is either a disk or an annulus. In contrast, rotational-invariant random normal matrix ensembles can have mean eigenvalue densities supported over any number of concentric annuli in the complex plane. In this paper we introduce and investigate, both analytically and numerically, a non-hermitian matrix model which flows from a generic matrix distribution obeying the Single Ring Theorem to a distribution of normal matrices by tuning a parameter which penalizes non-normality.

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) trained on large datasets and fine-tuned on downstream tasks have emerged as a powerful paradigm in AI for science. We present a systematic comparison of pre-training methods using the OmniLearned High Energy Physics FM framework. All models are pre-trained on the JetClass dataset and fine-tuned on two representative downstream tasks, top jet classification and JetNet conditional generation. Among other observations, for classification tasks, we find that pure classifier pre-training is optimal when downstream labels and model capacity are plentiful, but combining it with self-supervised masked particle modeling (MPM) is uniquely powerful in the low-finetuning label regime. Flow matching-based generative pre-training seems to provide little benefit for downstream classification, and interestingly, for downstream generation, we find that flow matching must be in the pre-training objective to see a significant finetuning advantage, hinting at the orthogonality of classification and generation tasks. That is, for a model to transfer to both generative and classification downstream tasks, it must be pre-trained on both. This study provides a template for controlled scaling analysis of pre-training objectives for foundation models in simulation-based sciences.

Abstract: Some cumulants of the fluctuations of conserved charges soon above the chiral crossover behave as if the quarks were free. This was taken by many as evidence of deconfinement. These correlators are qualitatively different from the free quark gas. Here we clarify the reason for the difference. The conserved quark number densities do not propagate in time but do propagate in spatial directions. The mesonic propagators calculated in full QCD differ radically from the free quark loop (quark gas) above T_ch. Therefore, we can reconstruct the spin density matrices, probe the quantum entanglement and Bell non-locality, and evade the no-go theorem. Abstract: A fast parton moving through the quark-gluon plasma trails a wake of color fields, usually characterized through its screening cloud and the associated energy loss. We address this within chiral kinetic theory with Berry curvature corrections, at finite $μ_5$ and zero magnetic field. The screening sector is found to be nearly insensitive to $μ_5$, which enters only through the Debye mass. The chiral imprint resides instead in a parity-odd sector, comprising an azimuthal field encircling the parton, a poloidal chromo-magnetic field, and a circulating color current. All three are linear in $μ_5$ and transverse to the velocity, and hence do no work on the parton. As the central result, these handed fields endow the wake with a net field angular momentum about the parton direction, odd in $μ_5$ and of order $10^{-4}$--$10^{-3}\,\hbar$ per parton. The chiral medium thereby opens a parity-odd channel connecting hard probes to the chiral and vortical structure of the plasma.

Abstract: Hadrons that decay predominantly into final states containing photons are notoriously difficult to detect at hadron colliders. Prominent examples are the yet-unobserved $B_{s0}^*$ and $B_{s1}$, the bottom partners of the $D_{s0}^*(2317)$ and $D_{s1}(2460)$, which are expected to exhibit exotic properties deviating from the conventional quark-model predictions of $\bar b s$ mesons. We propose a general, model-independent method to overcome this problem: when the target hadron has an attractive $S$-wave interaction with a companion hadron of precisely known mass, the line shape of a suitable final state develops a cusp at the pair threshold, or a peak just below it if the attraction binds, so that subtracting the companion mass returns the target mass, up to the binding energy in the latter case. Abstract: We construct a radiative neutrino mass model that combines the scotogenic mechanism with modular $S_4$ flavour symmetry. The entire lepton flavour structure is governed by holomorphic modular forms of a single complex modulus $τ$, eliminating the need for flavon fields. Beyond the Standard Model, the particle content consists of two right-handed Majorana fermions assigned to the $S_4$ doublet representation and an inert scalar doublet odd under a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ parity. Neutrino masses emerge at one loop through the scotogenic mechanism, and the lightest $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd state serves as a dark matter candidate. A comprehensive scan of the parameter space demonstrates consistency with all five neutrino oscillation observables at the $3σ$ level. Having exactly two right-handed neutrinos forces the light neutrino mass matrix to rank two, leaving one neutrino massless and selecting normal ordering as the only viable option. The modular structure of the right-handed Majorana mass matrix intrinsically produces a quasi-degenerate heavy neutrino spectrum, enabling flavoured resonant leptogenesis at $M_1 \sim 10^5\,\mathrm{GeV}$ without any fine-tuning. Integration of the full three-flavour Boltzmann equations confirms that the observed baryon asymmetry is reproduced, establishing that neutrino masses, leptonic mixing, and the baryon asymmetry of the Universe all find a common explanation within this framework.

Abstract: We present a dynamical mechanism for the erasure of inflationary isocurvature perturbations of the non-compact QCD axion. The key ingredient is an early-time runaway exponential potential, which drives the axion onto the well-known scaling cosmological attractor after inflation. Once on the attractor, the axion tracks the dominant component of the Universe, radiation, and isocurvature modes are erased even if the field is effectively massless during inflation. When the QCD potential turns on, the axion carries nonzero velocity, and kinetic misalignment can become operative. The exponential potential induces residual CP violation, potentially accessible to future electric dipole moment searches. This mechanism requires that the axion be effectively non-compact over the field range relevant for its post-inflationary evolution.

Method: 基于 phenomenological SU(3) flavor symmetry 和 final-state interaction (FSI) 理论，结合强子分子态唯象模型，计算B→[Q\bar q][Q'\bar q']类衰变的振幅与CP不对称参数。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the production of scalar singly charmed four-quark states from $B$ meson decays, employing phenomenological SU(3) flavor symmetry analysis and final-state interaction theory. Our study focuses on their production branching ratios and $CP$ violation. The results show that the branching ratios for producing either $VV$ or $PP$ molecular states from $B$ meson decays can be as large as the order of $10^{-4}$. Moreover, for processes with direct $CP$ violation, the production of $VV$ molecular states exhibits a sizable $CP$ asymmetry of order $10^{-3}$. Our findings also suggest that the recently observed $T_{\bar c\bar s 0}^*(2870)^0$ state is more consistent with the $\overline D^{*0} K^{*0}$ molecular configuration. We expect that our predictions will be validated by future experiments.

</details>

2. 复现Giombi et al. [arXiv:2007.04955] 的⟨φφ⟩体展开；
3. 首次给出⟨σσ⟩的体展开，由此导出未知OPE系数C_{σσσ³}的领头大N表达式；
4. 同时获得与Derkachov et al. [arXiv:hep-th/9705020] 一致的σ–σ³混合系数；

Conclusion: 谱函数方法是解析大N BCFT的有效框架，能系统提取边界临界数据；⟨σσ⟩的体展开为探测高阶单迹算符（如σ³）的OPE结构提供了新途径；当前结果支持O(N) BCFT在连续维度下的一致性，并为后续处理算符混合与更高圈修正奠定基础。

Abstract: The $\mathsf{O}(N)$ $\mathsf{BCFT}$ is analyzed in the large $N$ expansion in a generic bulk dimension $2<d+1<4$. Focus is on boundary conditions corresponding to the ordinary transition, however techniques used can be generalized to special or extraordinary transitions. We study the system of bulk $2$-pt functions $\langle φφ\rangle$, $\langle φ^2 φ^2\rangle$ and $\langle σσ\rangle$, where $σ$ is the Hubbard--Stratonovich field. They are expanded in bulk/boundary conformal partial waves. The coefficient functions of this expansion -- the spectral functions -- encode $\mathsf{BCFT}$ data and determine bulk/boundary conformal block expansions. We prove conjectures about boundary spectral functions in Dujava et al. [arXiv:2503.16345] and reproduce the bulk expansion of $\langle φφ\rangle$ in Giombi et al. [arXiv:2007.04955]. The bulk expansion of $\langleσσ\rangle$ is new and allows to extract the leading large $N$ expression for an unknown OPE coefficient $C_{σσσ^3}$ (the computation outputs as a byproduct also a mixing coefficient, which matches an existing result by Derkachov et al. [arXiv:hep-th/9705020], providing further support for the main result). Besides these derived data infinitely many constraints are produced, though due to operator mixing growing in complexity as degeneracy increases, they cannot be disentangled without further input.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Kerr-Sen类黑洞在Kerr/CFT框架下的普适热力学结构与对偶CFT描述，特别是其与Kerr-Newman黑洞的本质区别，并拓展至静态极限及准正规模物理。

Method: 基于DKSBH精确解的内/外视界热力学量（温度、熵、角速度等），计算熵积；结合Kerr/CFT对应假设，利用热力学第一定律与CFT Cardy公式反推中心荷；通过极限（a→0）过渡至dyonic dilaton BH；利用CFT-QNM对应关系（ω = T_L ± i(2n+1)T_R等）导出QNM频谱解析式。

Result: 1) DKSBH熵积 ℳ(S₊S₋) = 4π²J²，具普适性；2) CFT中心荷 c_L = c_R = 12J/ℏ，仅依赖J；3) 与Kerr-Newman相比，c_R相同但c_L结构不同，体现右行扇区主导差异；4) dyonic dilaton BH对应c_L = c_R = 12Q_P Q_M/ℏ ≠ 0；5) QNM频率 ω_{n,l,m} = mΩ_H − i2πT_H(n + 1/2 + iκ_l)，其中κ_l由BH参数解析确定。

Conclusion: DKSBH的热力学与Kerr/CFT对偶具有深层普适结构，其CFT描述由角动量完全刻画；该结构可连续过渡至静态解，并为QNM提供全息微观解释；结果支持Sen猜想中低能有效理论对偶性的稳健性。

Abstract: We explore the properties of inner and outer horizon thermodynamics of dyonic Kerr-Sen black hole (DKSBH). It is observed that the entropy (or area) product is universal, depending only on the angular momentum of the BH. We then proceed to study the dual conformal field theory~(CFT) in the Kerr/CFT correspondence using thermodynamic relations and compute the central charges from 2D CFT. The central charges are found to be universal with only angular momentum dependence. By comparing to Kerr-Newman BH, it is found that the essential difference is in the right-moving sector of the CFT. Interestingly, we can then explicitly produce the non-vanishing central charges related to its static solution, the dyonic dilaton BH, using the thermodynamic method. Moreover, from the CFT relations to multi-horizon thermodynamics, we find the analytical expression of the quasi-normal modes~(QNM) spectra in terms of BH parameters.

</details>

Method: 基于Euler-Lagrange方程构建有效变分方法推导场方程；在FLRW背景上求解de Sitter解；采用动力学系统方法（线性扰动+相空间分析）进行稳定性判别。

Result: 发现场方程中存在未被原始文献识别的高阶导数项差异，但在de Sitter背景下该差异自动消失，故解相同；动力学分析显示该de Sitter解在所有参数范围内均为鞍点型不稳定。

Conclusion: Einstein-Grisaru-Zanon引力中的de Sitter解本质上不稳定，不支持长期暴胀或晚期加速，验证并强化了原工作的稳定性结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we would like to investigate the stability of de Sitter solution in the Einstein-Grisaru-Zanon gravity, which is a novel fourth-order gravity model considered recently in a paper [Phys. Lett. B {\bf 855} (2024) 138811]. As a result, we are able to derive the corresponding field equations for the Einstein-Grisaru-Zanon gravity by using an effective method based on the Euler-Lagrange equations. Unfortunately, one of the obtained field equations does not coincide with that derived in the original paper of the Einstein-Grisaru-Zanon gravity due to a gap between higher-order derivative terms. However, our de Sitter solution is still identical to one solved in the original paper of the Einstein-Grisaru-Zanon gravity due to the vanishing of the gap. Furthermore, a stability analysis based on the dynamical system method is performed to indicate that the obtained de Sitter solution is always unstable, no matter it presents an inflationary phase or expanding phase of universe. This result confirms the validity of stability investigation carried out in the original paper of the Einstein-Grisaru-Zanon gravity.

</details>

Method: 引入多熵向量作为扩展坐标系，利用多收缩映射构造全息多熵不等式的严格判定工具，并结合凸几何与群作用分类n=3,4情形下所有极 facet，进而归纳结构规律。

Conclusion: 全息多熵结构具有严格的凸几何表征，HMEC比HEC承载更丰富的纠缠层级信息，其facet结构揭示了全息态中多体纠缠受更强、更精细的几何约束。

Abstract: We generalize the holographic entropy cone (HEC) to the holographic multi-entropy cone (HMEC) by adjoining multi-entropy coordinates to the standard bipartition entropy coordinates. We show that holographic states, through their multi-entropy vectors, form a rational polyhedral cone in multi-entropy space, and multicontraction maps provide exact certificates for holographic multi-entropy inequalities (HMEIs). We determine all facets of the $n=3,4$ HMECs, where $n$ includes the purifier, and obtain seven fundamental HMEI orbits: two for $n=3$ and five for $n=4$. We further propose two structural conjectures: HEC facet inequalities are convex combinations of HMEC facet inequalities, and HMEC facets obey a balanced-but-not-too-balanced principle.

</details>

Abstract: We further investigate the 3d gauged linear sigma model (GLSM)/quantum K-theory correspondence for partial flag manifolds $X \equiv {\rm Fl}(\boldsymbol{k};n)$. This is a 3d uplift of the 2d GLSM/quantum cohomology correspondence with the 3d theory compactified on $\mathbb{R}^2\times S^1_β$. Recently, a set of half-BPS line operators, called Schubert line defects, were constructed that correspond to the Schubert classes in the K-theory ring of $X$. Utilizing algebro-geometric algorithms, we compute $2$-point and $3$-point correlation functions of these line operators in the 3d A-model regime of the theory. These are interpreted as genus-$0$ K-theoretic Gromov--Witten invariants, and they produce the K-theoretic Littlewood--Richardson coefficients of the quantum K-theory ring of $X$. We show how this works explicitly in examples. Taking the small $β$ limit, we apply these techniques to the resulting 2d GLSM. We explicitly compute the quantum cohomology ring relations of $X$ for some cases and match with existing results in the literature in examples.

</details>


### [97] [Generalized Schwarzian Dynamics from a Bulk-First BF Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15270)
*H. T. Özer,Aytül Filiz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从二维BF引力的体理论出发，通过Drinfeld-Sokolov约化，导出边界上的广义Schwarzian动力学：sl(2,R)情形复现标准Schwarzian作用量，sl(3,R)情形则由第二、第三Wilczynski不变量描述，构成Schwarzian导数的高秩推广；该动力学直接源于平直BF联络及其伴随形式，并与Casimir荷、单值性数据及广义Schwarzian热力学（含单值谱与半经典热力学）紧密关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一种不预设边界理论、而从体理论（BF引力）自然导出广义Schwarzian动力学的‘体优先’框架，统一理解其几何起源（射影几何）、对称性约化（Drinfeld-Sokolov）与热力学结构。

Method: 基于二维BF引力模型，系统分析其渐近相空间结构，实施Drinfeld-Sokolov约化以提取边界动力学；对sl(2,R)和sl(3,R)情况分别计算约化后的作用量，识别Wilczynski不变量；进一步将所得射影不变量与Casimir算符、单值矩阵特征值及热力学量（如熵、温度）关联。

Result: 1) sl(2,R) BF引力在特定渐近扇区给出标准Schwarzian作用量；2) sl(3,R)情形导出由第二、第三Wilczynski不变量主导的广义Schwarzian动力学；3) 射影不变量恒定条件精确对应特定Casimir扇区与单值性数据；4) 建立广义Schwarzian热力学的半经典描述，其中单值谱决定热力学态密度。

Conclusion: 广义Schwarzian动力学是二维平直BF引力在Drinfeld-Sokolov约化下的内在边界现象，其几何本质为射影不变量，物理内涵涵盖渐近对称性、Casimir分类与边界热力学，从而实现体理论、射影几何与量子引力低能有效理论的统一描述。

Abstract: We investigate the emergence of generalized Schwarzian dynamics from a bulk-first BF perspective. Starting from two-dimensional BF gravity, we analyze the associated boundary phase space and its Drinfeld-Sokolov reductions. For the sl(2,R) theory, we recover the ordinary Schwarzian action as the reduced boundary dynamics arising from a particular sector of the BF asymptotic phase space. We then extend this construction to sl(3,R), where the reduced dynamics is governed by the second and third Wilczynski invariants, providing a natural higher-rank generalization of the Schwarzian derivative. In this framework, generalized Schwarzian dynamics emerges directly from flat BF connections and their companion forms rather than being introduced as an independent boundary theory. We further relate the resulting projective invariants to Casimir charges, monodromy data, and generalized Schwarzian thermodynamics, including monodromy spectra and semiclassical thermodynamics. In particular, constant projective invariants determine the corresponding Casimir sectors and monodromy data, which in turn organize the thermodynamic structure of the theory. Our results provide a unified bulk-first description of Schwarzian and generalized Schwarzian dynamics and reveal a direct link between BF gravity, asymptotic symmetry reductions, projective geometry, and boundary thermodynamics.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Corrections to Page Curve of Charged Near-AdS$_2$ Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15562)
*Zi-Qing Xiao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定温度和化学势下，耦合到非引力浴的带电近AdS₂黑洞的蒸发过程与Page曲线。通过平均Schwarzian重参数化模和U(1)相位模对边界能量与出射流的影响，修正了热力学平衡方程，并据此计算无岛与有岛熵及Page时间偏移；发现U(1)模延迟Page转变，Schwarzian模提前之，总Page时间偏移由二者竞争决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低维带电黑洞在非引力浴中量子纠缠演化与信息恢复机制，特别是软模式（Schwarzian与U(1)相位）对Page曲线及信息悖论解决路径的影响。

Method: 基于近AdS₂引力理论，对Schwarzian重参数化模与U(1)规范相位模进行系综平均，导出修正的能流与电荷流平衡方程；在此有效背景上，采用量子极值面公式计算无岛与岛贡献的广义熵，并提取Page时间偏移。

Result: U(1)相位模使Page时间推迟，Schwarzian模使其提前；二者竞争导致净Page时间偏移依赖于相对耦合强度与温度标度；在低温半经典区域，软模式显著调制Page转变动力学。

Conclusion: Schwarzian与U(1)软模式并非被动背景，而是主动参与并调控黑洞信息释放时序；Page曲线的精细结构需同时纳入引力与规范软模的协同效应。

Abstract: We study the evaporation and Page curve of charged near-AdS$_2$ black holes coupled to a non-gravitating bath at fixed temperature and chemical potential. The low-energy dynamics is governed by the Schwarzian reparametrization mode together with a $U(1)$ phase mode. We average the boundary energy and the outgoing flux over these two soft modes and obtain corrected balance equations for the temperature and chemical potential. We then use the corrected background to calculate the no-island and island entropies and the Page time shifts. We find that the two soft sectors affect the Page transition in our low-temperature semiclassical regime. The $U(1)$ phase mode correction delays the Page transition, while the Schwarzian correction tends to move it earlier. The total Page time shift is therefore determined by the competition between the Schwarzian and $U(1)$ sectors.

</details>


### [99] [Strong primordial inhomogeneities of axion-like field in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15661)

Method: 构建含复标量场（具Mexican hat势）与Gauss-Bonnet项耦合的有效作用量，在慢滚暴胀背景下求解背景演化与线性扰动，分析相变动力学、功率谱增强机制、轴子质量谱、黑洞形成阈值及张量扰动谱。

Result: 暴胀期间发生U(1)对称性恢复与破缺相变；小尺度原初扰动谱显著蓝倾斜（n_s ≫ 1），激发超轻类轴子（m_a ∼ 10^{-22}–10^{-20} eV）；在无回响近似下PBH无法形成；但通过增强张量模涨落可在f ∼ 1–10 nHz产生Ω_GW h^2 ∼ 10^{-10}量级引力波，匹配NANOGrav信号。

Conclusion: 该模型为统一解释纳赫兹引力波、模糊暗物质与原初黑洞缺失提供自洽框架，凸显高阶曲率耦合在早期宇宙相变动力学中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this paper we consider complex scalar field with Mexican hat potential coupled with Gauss-Bonnet scalar resulting in restored U(1) symmetry at the stage of inflation, corresponding to the modern cosmological horizon. In our model, the phase transition takes place during inflation, avoiding large-scale axion isocurvature perturbations. Our model provides strong primordial inhomogeneities on small scales and ultra-light axion-like particle as either a fuzzy dark matter candidate or a candidate for inhomogeneous dark energy. We also study the possibility of black hole formation and gravitational wave background in this scenario. We find that in the case when the symmetry breaking field does not backreact on inflation, black hole production is not feasible. However, under specific conditions, gravitational waves fitting the NANOGrav band can be obtained.

</details>


### [100] [Flat Gauging of Continuous (Non-invertible) Symmetries and Non-compact BF SymTFT for Compact Boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15732)
*Qiang Jia,Yi Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续对称性的平直规范（flat gauging），聚焦于二维紧致玻色子模型，构建了具有平直U(1)_M × U(1)_W 背景的环面配分函数；发现平直规范任一U(1)因子会导致理论退紧致化为非紧致自由玻色子，并揭示其与混合反常相关的对偶Z背景与剩余U(1)背景组合成非紧致R对称性背景；在自对偶半径处，平直规范SO(3)子群导出连续orbifold，超出标准c=1模空间；进一步分析orbifold分支上的有限与连续不可逆平直规范，并指出连续情形需处理模空间上零测度不动点的处方；最后将环面σ模型的SymTFT表述为非紧致BF理论，其拓扑边界态编码Narain模空间及O(D,D;Z) T-对偶作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解平直规范作为构造新量子场论（尤其是非可逆对称性与连续orbifold）的工具，厘清其在c=1共形场论、T-对偶性与对称性分类中的作用，并统一描述Narain模空间的拓扑场论实现。

Method: 使用路径积分方法对平直规范场构型求和；显式计算二维紧致玻色子在U(1)_M×U(1)_W平直背景下的环面配分函数；结合对偶性、反常匹配与SymTFT框架，分析SO(3)平直规范与连续orbifold结构；引入边界条件与拓扑边界态刻画Narain模空间。

Result: 1. 平直规范单个U(1)因子导致退紧致化；2. 混合反常使对偶Z背景与剩余U(1)合成R背景；3. 自对偶半径下SO(3)平直规范生成连续orbifold，位于c=1模空间之外；4. 连续不可逆平直规范需特殊处理模空间零测度不动点；5. 环面σ模型的SymTFT等价于非紧致BF理论，其拓扑边界态实现O(D,D;Z)作用。

Conclusion: 平直规范不仅是规范对称性的技术手段，更是探索非可逆对称性、连续orbifold与广义对偶性的基本构造机制；SymTFT框架为Narain理论提供了自然的拓扑分类，并将T-对偶升格为边界条件的对称性。

Abstract: We study flat gauging of continuous symmetries by summing over flat gauge-field configurations. We focus on the two-dimensional compact boson and construct the torus partition function with general flat $U(1)_M\times U(1)_W$ backgrounds. We show that flat gauging either $U(1)_M$ or $U(1)_W$ decompactifies the theory to the non-compact free boson, and that the dual $\mathbb{Z}$ background combines with the remaining $U(1)$ background into a non-compact $\mathbb{R}$ symmetry background due to the mixed anomaly. We also revisit the self-dual radius, where flat gauging the diagonal $SO(3)\subset (SU(2)_L\times SU(2)_R)/\mathbb{Z}_2$, first pointed out by Gaberdiel and Suchanek, gives the continuous orbifold which lies outside the usual $c=1$ moduli space. On the orbifold branch, we study finite and continuous non-invertible flat gaugings and explain why the continuous case requires a prescription for zero-measure fixed loci on the moduli space. Finally, we formulate the SymTFT of torus sigma models as a non-compact BF theory, whose topological boundary states encode the Narain moduli space and the $O(D,D;\mathbb{Z})$ T-duality action.

</details>


### [101] [Entanglement islands of 2D charged scalar-hairy black holes on the brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15792)
*Qingsong Li,Yuxuan Liu,Wenjie Zhu*

TL;DR: 本文研究双全息框架下2D膜上Maxwell-Scalar黑洞与热浴耦合时的纠缠动力学与Page曲线，发现电荷注入提升膜上自由度并提高饱和熵，而标量源则降低自由度并抑制纠缠；纠缠熵在预饱和阶段呈现早期二次增长（对应预局域热化）和晚期线性增长，Page时间尺度由热激发与膜自由度之间的竞争决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双全息背景下带物质场的低维黑洞在热环境中的量子纠缠演化，特别是Page曲线如何受不同物质场（Maxwell场与标量场）背反调控的影响。

Method: 基于岛公式（island formula）计算膜上边界态的纠缠熵，结合双全息对偶，在2D brane Maxwell-Scalar黑洞背景中分析电荷与标量源对有效张力及红外几何的非平凡背反应。

Result: 电荷增强有效全局张力、提升膜自由度与饱和熵；标量源削弱有效局域张力、抑制纠缠；纠缠动力学在预饱和阶段稳健：早期t²增长（预局域热化），晚期t增长；Page时间∝ S_sat / T，由饱和熵与温度竞争决定。

Conclusion: Maxwell与标量场通过调控膜上张力与自由度对纠缠结构施加背反影响，但整体Page曲线形态（二次→线性增长→饱和）保持普适性，揭示了低维双全息黑洞中热化与信息恢复的张力平衡机制。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement dynamics and Page curve of a 2D brane Maxwell-Scalar (MS) black hole coupled to thermal baths in double holography. Computing the entanglement entropy via the island formula, we find that injecting electric charge and scalar source into the system produce opposite effects: the former increases the degrees of freedom (DOF) on the brane by enhancing the effective global tension, thereby raising the saturation entropy of the system; conversely, the latter reduces the DOF on the brane by lowering the effective local tension near the IR geometry, ultimately suppressing the entanglement. Despite the non-trivial backreaction from the matter fields, the pre-saturation entanglement dynamics remain robust. We identify an early-time quadratic growth corresponding to the pre-local-thermalization phase, which smoothly transitions into a late-time linear growth. Furthermore, the scale of the Page time is governed by a competition between thermal excitations and the brane DOF. Higher temperatures accelerate the onset of the Page time by exciting more Hawking modes, while abundant DOF delay it by increasing the saturated entropy.

</details>


### [102] [An integrable deformation of the sine(sinh)-Gordon model -- Malcev algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15794)
*Meseret Asrat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类二维可积模型，作为正弦（双曲正弦）-Gordon模型的变形；构造了其Lax联络与经典r-矩阵，验证其满足经典Yang-Baxter方程；代数结构基于Z-分次非Lie Malcev代数，该代数为若干sl(2,ℝ)的直和，共享同一Cartan子代数；Malcev代数对应解析Moufang环的单位元切空间；作者预期该模型在量子层面亦可积，并给出一族与二维Poisson-Boltzmann方程相关的经典可积模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有非Lie代数结构（Malcev代数）的新二维可积场论，拓展经典可积系统代数基础，并探索其与物理方程（如Poisson-Boltzmann方程）的联系。

Method: 通过形变sine-Gordon/sinh-Gordon模型构建新模型；显式给出Lax联络；推导并验证其经典r-矩阵满足经典Yang-Baxter方程；识别底层代数结构为Z-分次Malcev代数，具体实现为sl(2,ℝ)直和配共享Cartan；建立与Poisson-Boltzmann方程的类比映射。

Result: 确认该模型为经典可积场论；首次将Malcev代数作为可积模型的对称代数结构明确实现；给出一族相关可积模型，其相容性由Poisson-Boltzmann型方程保障。

Conclusion: 该变形模型是经典可积的，其代数框架突破了传统Lie代数限制，指向更广义的代数结构（Malcev代数）在可积系统中的作用；为量子可积性及Moufang环几何在场论中的应用提供了新路径。

Abstract: In this paper we present an integrable model in two dimensions. It is a deformation of the sine(sinh)-Gordon model. We give its Lax connection. We also obtain its (classical) $r$-matrix. It satisfies the classical Yang-Baxter equation. Thus, the model is a classical integrable field theory in two dimensions. The underlying algebra is a ${\cal Z}$-graded non-Lie Malcev algebra. It is a direct sum of $sl(2, {\rm I\!R})$ Lie algebras with a shared common Cartan. A Malcev algebra is the tangent space at the identity of an analytic Moufang loop as a Lie algebra is the tangent space at the identity of a Lie group. We expect the model to be integrable at the quantum level. We also give a family of classically integrable models which are related to the Poisson-Boltzmann equation in two dimensions.

</details>


### [103] [Dressed Fock Spaces in Gauge Theory and Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15988)
*Sangmin Choi,Prahar Mitra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，四维规范和引力理论中的红外发散问题并非源于多粒子态无法分解为单粒子态的张量积，而是由于选择了不恰当的红外自由度；通过引入新的辐射场零模和利用渐近对称性的戈德斯通模式，可重构红外有限的 dressed 态，并恢复标准福克空间的因子化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Faddeev-Kulish dressed态破坏Fock空间因子化的概念困境，厘清红外发散的物理起源（软光子/引力子云 vs 渐近对称性戈德斯通模式与新零模）

Method: 采用渐近对称性分析，将软辐射自由度重新参数化：用BMS或大规范变换的戈德斯通模式刻画真实软发散，用新引入的辐射场零模刻画库仑相位；在此新变量下构造红外有限且可因子化的dressed态

Result: 证明存在一组物理上等价但数学上更自然的红外自由度，使得红外有限的dressed多粒子态严格满足福克空间张量积结构，即 |Ψ_dressed⟩ = ⊗_i |ψ_i,dressed⟩

Conclusion: Fock空间因子化的丧失是人为坐标选择的结果，而非红外物理的本质；真正的红外结构由渐近对称性破缺所支配，其戈德斯通模式与新零模共同完整编码软辐射效应。

Abstract: Four-dimensional gauge and gravitational theories exhibit long-range interactions that require asymptotic particles to be dressed by clouds of soft photons and gravitons. Faddeev-Kulish dressings render scattering amplitudes infrared-finite, but the resulting multi-particle states do not factorise into tensor products of dressed one-particle states. We show that this loss of Fock-space factorisation is not fundamental, but reflects an inappropriate choice of infrared variables. The real soft divergence is reproduced by the Goldstone modes of asymptotic symmetries, while the Coulomb phase is reproduced by new zero modes of the radiative fields that we introduce here. In these variables, infrared-finite dressed multi-particle states admit the usual Fock-space factorisation into single-particle dressed states.

</details>


### [104] [The O(4)-breaking bubble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16024)
*Guy Avraham,Kfir Blum,Omri Rosner,Isaac G. Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个在标量场理论中具有O(2)×O(2)对称性的非径向假真空衰变解（双环泡管结构），突破了传统Coleman O(4)-对称bounce的唯一性假设，揭示了新一类具有奇数不稳定模的隧穿通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验假真空衰变是否严格由Coleman O(4)-对称bounce主导；探索数学上已知但物理上未实现的O(4)-破缺临界点解的存在性与动力学意义。

Method: 在六次有界下方势V(φ)=m²φ²/2−λφ⁴/4+gφ⁶/6中，通过显式构造满足欧氏运动方程的非径向、O(2)×O(2)×Z₂对称解，验证其作为有效Cauchy初值的合法性，并分析其Morse指标（不稳定模数）。

Result: 首次显式构造出具有O(2)×O(2)旋转对称性加环交换宇称的双泡管解；该解具有有限作用量、实时间可延拓性，且Morse指标为奇数，表明其对应非平凡隧穿路径。

Conclusion: Coleman bounce并非假真空衰变的唯一主导通道；存在具有更低对称性、奇数不稳定模的新型临界点解，可能影响真空衰变速率与早期宇宙相变动力学。

Abstract: False vacuum decay in field theory is thought to be dominated by Coleman's O(4)-symmetric bounce, the minimum action nontrivial solution of the imaginary time equations of motion. Beyond the bounce, non-constructive existence proofs of O(4)-breaking solutions are available in the mathematics literature, but the solutions themselves, and their physics, have remained unknown. Considering the simple, bounded-below, scalar field potential $V(φ)=\frac{m^2}{2}φ^2-\fracλ{4}φ^4+\frac{g}{6}φ^6$, we construct a nonradial solution explicitly: two bubble-tubes of opposite sign wrapping orthogonal rings, invariant under ${\rm O}(2)\times{\rm O}(2)$ rotations combined with a parity that exchanges the rings. The solution admits valid Cauchy data for real time evolution from a $t=0$ slice, and supports an odd number of unstable deformation modes.

</details>


### [105] [Effect of $ξRφ^2$ non-minimal coupling on gravitational light bending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16406)
*Ayan Kumar Naskar,Avijit Sen Majumder,Sourav Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在曲率-标量非最小耦合√(-g)ξRφ²存在下，无质量场（标量场和光子）被大质量天体（如恒星）弯曲的量子引力效应，至一阶圈图修正；采用微扰量子引力与eikonal近似，发现弯曲角主导项为O(ξG²)，且随碰撞参数b呈b⁻⁷依赖，显著区别于最小耦合情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自相互作用标量场在引力背景中不可重整的问题，通过引入非最小耦合项√(-g)ξRφ²实现可重整性，并探究该耦合对经典与量子散射角的修正效应。

Method: 采用微扰量子引力框架，在非相对论性大质量标量源（建模为恒星）背景下，计算无质量标量场与光子的2→2散射振幅；利用eikonal近似，在ξ与弯曲角均为小量假设下，提取至O(ξG²)阶的散射相位并导出偏转角。

Result: 发现树图阶O(ξG)无偏转贡献，首阶非零偏转出现在一阶圈图O(ξG²)；偏转角∝ b⁻⁷，源于非最小耦合顶点含动量依赖的引力子外线结构，与标准最小耦合顶点√G h_μν T^μν有本质区别。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合√(-g)ξRφ²在量子引力散射中产生独特的高阶动量依赖顶点，导致偏转角呈现异常强的冲击参数依赖（b⁻⁷），为检验超越最小耦合的引力-物质相互作用提供了可观测的量子效应判据。

Abstract: We investigate the bending of massless fields by a massive object in the presence of a curvature-scalar $\sqrt{-g}ξR φ^2$ non-minimal coupling up to one loop, using the perturbative quantum gravity computations. It is well known that without such coupling a self interacting scalar field theory cannot be renormalised in the presence of gravity. The massive object is modelled by a massive scalar $φ$, and it is assumed to be non-relativistic, e.g., a star. We compute the 2-2 scattering of massless scalar and photons off this object via graviton exchanges. Assuming both $ξ$ and the bending angle to be small, we use the eikonal approximation to compute the angle up to ${\cal O}(ξG^2)$. At tree level $({\cal O}(ξG))$ we find no bending, and hence the ${\cal O}(ξG^2)$ result happens to be leading in this case. The non-minimal vertices are qualitatively different from that of the standard minimal ones, e.g. $ \sqrt{G} h_{μν} T^{μν}$, as the former contains explicit momenta of the gravitons instead of the scalar, complementing the second. The bending angle is found to behave like $\sim b^{-7}$, where $b$ is the impact parameter. We have emphasised the qualitative differences of our results from that of the well studied minimal case.

</details>


### [106] [Complexified Virasoro Flow and the Logarithmic Graviton at the Chiral Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16444)
*Yannick Mvondo-She*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于复化Virasoro演化的几何视角，解释拓扑质量引力中chiral点处对数引力子所对应的秩二Jordan结构，并揭示径向演化、时间演化、Jordan混合与对数单值性在统一复参数s下的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS₃/LCFT₂对应中对数引力子的几何本质及其与边界对数共形场论中对数伙伴的匹配机制；澄清chiral点处退化模与Jordan块的物理起源。

Method: 从Grumiller-Johansson对数引力子出发，分析其对数系数y(τ,ρ)在AdS₃边界的渐近展开；引入复参数s=ρ+iτ，将其与Virasoro生成元L₀在秩二Jordan胞上的指数作用e^{sL₀}=e^{sh}(1+sN)关联；通过复化Virasoro流统一描述径向演化、时间演化、对数混合与单值性。

Result: 证明对数系数y(τ,ρ)的渐近形式精确对应于复化Virasoro演化参数s；确立Jordan演化e^{sL₀}自动再生对数扇区的特征混合行为；发现s→s+2πi解析延拓即产生对数单值性（logarithmic monodromy）；指出径向、时间、Jordan混合与单值性均为同一复化Virasoro流的不同表现。

Conclusion: 对数共形场论中的不可分解结构具有明确的几何实现——即复化Virasoro群作用下的Jordan演化；该框架为AdS₃/LCFT₂猜想提供了更深刻的几何基础，并重新诠释了对数引力子作为复流之不变量的本质角色。

Abstract: At the chiral point of topologically massive gravity, the massive graviton becomes degenerate with the left-moving graviton, leading to the appearance of a logarithmic mode and a corresponding rank-two Jordan structure. This logarithmic graviton plays a central role in the conjectured AdS$_3$/LCFT$_2$ correspondence, where it has been widely interpreted as the bulk counterpart of a logarithmic partner in the putative boundary logarithmic conformal field theory. In this work, we develop a geometric interpretation of this Jordan structure based on complexified Virasoro evolution. Starting from the logarithmic graviton of Grumiller and Johansson, we show that its logarithmic coefficient \[ y(τ,ρ) =
-iτ-\ln\coshρ\] admits, near the AdS$_3$ boundary, the asymptotic form \[ y(τ,ρ) =
-s+\ln2+\mathcal O(e^{-2ρ}), \qquad s=ρ+iτ. \] The same complex parameter naturally appears in the exponentiation of the Virasoro generator $L_0$ acting on a rank-two Jordan cell, \[ L_0=h\mathbf1+N, \qquad N^2=0, \] for which \[ e^{sL_0} =
e^{sh}(1+sN). \] We show that the resulting Jordan evolution reproduces the characteristic logarithmic mixing of the logarithmic sector, while analytic continuation $s\rightarrow s+2πi$ generates the corresponding logarithmic monodromy. From this perspective, radial evolution, temporal evolution, Jordan mixing, and logarithmic monodromy may be viewed as different manifestations of a single complexified Virasoro flow. The analysis suggests a geometric interpretation of the indecomposable structures characteristic of logarithmic conformal field theory and offers a new perspective on the logarithmic graviton within the conjectural AdS$_3$/LCFT$_2$ framework.

</details>


### [107] [Self-gravitating electromagnetic waves in the dark bubble model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16547)
*Ulf Danielsson,Daniel Panizo,Vincent Van Hemelryck*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了在AdS5中基于pp波几何的引力与电磁波嵌入，通过在三维膜上拼接两个AdS5 pp波时空实现；应用该构造分析局域光束及其引力反作用，并在AdS5中施加适当混合边界条件，发现引力修正导致四维引力在五维AdS尺度上减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pp波时空常为运动方程的精确解，故被用于构造暗泡（dark bubble）场景下引力与电磁波的嵌入。

Method: 采用AdS5中的pp波几何，通过三维膜拼接两个AdS5 pp波时空；对局域光束施加AdS5混合边界条件以计算其引力反作用。

Result: 得到与四维引力在五维AdS尺度上减弱一致的引力修正；验证了局域电磁辐射在该背景下的自洽耦合行为。

Conclusion: 暗泡模型可通过pp波AdS5嵌入实现，并支持弱化四维引力的低能有效描述，为额外维引力屏蔽机制提供具体几何实现。

Abstract: We construct embeddings of gravitational and electromagnetic waves in the dark bubble scenario using pp-wave geometries in AdS5, motivated by the fact that pp-wave spacetimes often provide exact solutions to the equations of motion. The setup is realised by gluing two AdS5 pp-wave spacetimes across a three-brane. As an application, we analyse localised beams of light and their gravitational backreaction. Imposing suitable mixed boundary conditions in AdS5, we find gravitational corrections consistent with a weakening of 4d gravity at the 5d AdS scale.

</details>


### [108] [The supergravity dual of a finite duality cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16630)
*Pietro Moroni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了含Orientifold平面的N=1超对称规范理论中的有限级联RG流，构造了其对应的超引力对偶解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Orientifold平面如何改变Klebanov-Strassler型级联RG流的无穷性，实现有限步数的对偶描述。

Method: 基于D3-膜与分数D3-膜在奇点上的堆叠构型，引入O3/O7平面破缺部分超对称性，并求解IIB型超引力中相应的BPS背景。

Result: 构造出一个解析的、各向异性且渐近AdS的超引力解，精确刻画了有限步RG级联、UV有限自由度及IR软化行为。

Conclusion: Orientifold的存在使RG级联从无限变为有限，表明对偶引力描述中拓扑与对称性破缺可调控量子场论的重整化群结构。

Abstract: This proceedings contribution summarizes the main results of the original paper arXiv:2506.18988. Cascading RG flows are characteristic of $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories realized by D3-branes probing singularities in the presence of fractional branes. A celebrated example is the Klebanov-Strassler model, which exhibits an infinite cascade that ends with confinement. In this work, we explore a related setup where the addition of an orientifold plane modifies the cascade structure: the RG flow now consists of a finite number of steps, originating from a UV fixed point with a finite number of degrees of freedom. We provide a supergravity solution dual to this flow, that reproduces all its salient features.

</details>


### [109] [Flux Quantization on 10D Type IIA Superspace via Cyclification from 11D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16857)
*Grigorios Giotopoulos,Hisham Sati*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过环化（cyclification）将11维超引力在超扭率自由超空间上仅由C场对偶对称Bianchi恒等式即可确定其壳上结构的结果，约化至10维IIA型超引力；其核心机制是：duality-symmetric Bianchi恒等式由分类空间𝒜的Whitehead括号L∞-代数𝔩𝒜刻画，而其维数约化则由循环环空间Cyc(𝒜)刻画；对11D情形，𝒜=S⁴，故10D IIA超引力壳上等价于在supertorsion-free超时空上施加𝔩Cyc(S⁴)-Bianchi恒等式于NS/RR超通量密度；由此导出Cyc(S⁴)的ℝ-有理同伦等价空间即为允许的非阿贝尔上同调系数空间，从而给出可氧化回11D的全局完备化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立超引力理论在不同维度间维数约化的数学统一框架，特别是兼容非阿贝尔上同调与通量量子化，并实现红外全局完成与氧化可能性的协同刻画。

Method: 运用L∞-代数、Whitehead括号、超空间上对偶对称Bianchi恒等式、环化(cyclification)函子及ℝ-有理同伦论，将11D超引力的壳上结构通过分类空间S⁴的环化Cyc(S⁴)实现向10D IIA的系统性约化。

Result: 证明10D IIA超引力壳上动力学完全由𝔩Cyc(S⁴)-Bianchi恒等式在supertorsion-free超时空上约束NS/RR超通量密度所决定；Cyc(S⁴)及其ℝ-有理等价类给出了适配氧化至11D的非阿贝尔通量量子化系数空间。

Conclusion: 维数约化可统一理解为分类空间的环化操作，且该机制天然兼容超引力的全局非阿贝尔上同调完成；11D→10D IIA约化对应S⁴→Cyc(S⁴)，确立了通量量子化与氧化可能性之间的拓扑-代数对应。

Abstract: We produce the dimensional reduction to 10D IIA supergravity (SuGra), via cyclification, of the remarkable result that full 11D SuGra is put on shell just by imposing the duality-symmetric Bianchi identities on C-field super-flux densities over supertorsion-free superspace.
  Generally, we highlight that when duality-symmetric superspace Bianchi identities are characterized by Whitehead bracket $L_\infty$-algebras $\mathfrak{l}\mathcal{A}$ of a classifying space $\mathcal{A}$, their dimensional reduction is characterized by the cyclic loop space $\mathrm{Cyc}(\mathcal{A})$. We promote this to a general mechanism of dimensional reduction on super-spacetime, compatible with the global (infrared) completion of supergravity theories by flux quantization in non-abelian cohomology with coefficients in $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathrm{Cyc}(\mathcal{A})$, respectively.
  In the case of 11D SuGra, the characteristic $L_\infty$-algebra is $\mathfrak{l}S^4$ and hence we obtain that full on-shell 10D IIA SuGra is equivalent to $\mathfrak{l}\mathrm{Cyc}(S^4)$-Bianchi imposed identities on NS/RR super-flux densities over supertorsion-free 10D super-spacetime. This implies that any space which is $\mathbb{R}$-rationally equivalent to $\mathrm{Cyc}(S^4)$ classifies an admissible flux quantization law, which provides a global completion of 10D IIA SuGra that admits oxidation to 11D.

</details>


### [110] [Holographic study of heavy quark potential, free energy, and running coupling in backgrounds with broken translational symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16640)
*Wenxing Cheng,Zi-qiang Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用五维渐近AdS时空模型（含平移对称性破缺）研究重夸克观测量（静态夸克势、热自由能、跑动耦合常数），揭示有限重子密度（化学势μ）与动量弛豫（TSB参数β）通过不同机制削弱色相互作用并促进夸克偶素解离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）中重夸克探针的介质修正机制，特别是有限重子密度与动量耗散对色相互作用及热力学性质的独立影响。

Method: 基于Einstein-Maxwell-axion引力对偶模型，在具有平移对称性破缺（TSB）的五维渐近AdS时空中进行数值模拟，分别分析有限/零温下化学势μ与TSB参数β对静态夸克势、热自由能及跑动耦合的影响。

Result: μ主导色屏蔽效应，显著修改重夸克势与跑动耦合；β主要影响等离子体熵并修正热自由能；零温下热自由能退化为介质修正的静态势（近库仑形式）；有限重子密度对Q\bar{Q}束缚的压制远强于动量耗散；提取了色屏蔽长度与解离尺度。

Conclusion: μ与β通过不同物理通道调控色相互作用：前者增强色屏蔽，后者调制热力学响应；该对偶框架为致密耗散QGP中的重夸克探针提供了自洽的全息描述。

Abstract: We study heavy-quark observables including static interquark potential, thermal free energy and running coupling via a five-dimensional asymptotically AdS spacetime with translational symmetry breaking (TSB). The Einstein-Maxwell-axion geometry involves two scales: chemical potential $μ$ for finite baryon density, and TSB parameter $β$ for momentum relaxation. Numerical simulations at finite and zero temperature reveal that both $μ$ and $β$ weaken color interactions and facilitate quarkonium dissociation in strongly coupled quark-gluon plasmas through different mechanisms. The chemical potential dominates color screening and modifies the heavy-quark potential and running coupling, while $β$ mainly affects plasma entropy and corrects thermal free energy. At zero temperature, thermal contributions vanish, and the renormalized free energy becomes a medium-modified static potential with an approximate Coulombic form. Finite baryon density suppresses $Q\bar{Q}$ binding much more strongly than momentum dissipation at all temperatures. We extract the color screening length and dissociation scale, and discuss phenomenological implications for quarkonium in heavy-ion collisions. This work clarifies medium correction mechanisms for color interactions and thermodynamics, and presents a consistent picture for heavy-quark probes in dense dissipative plasmas.

</details>


### [111] [On the Representation Theory of Non-Admissible $W$-Algebras: Part I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16714)
*Dan Xie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义仿射Springer纤维几何的框架，用于研究非可容许W-代数W^k(g,f)在特定临界水平附近的表示论，将C*不动点轨道与简单模的最高权及对数模结构联系起来，并在多个李代数型（D4, E6, E8及扭型^3D4, ^2A3）中验证了该对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受圆紧化4维N=2理论镜像对称性的启发，旨在为非可容许W-代数在临界水平附近的表示论建立几何实现。

Method: 利用斜率ν=u/m的广义仿射Springer纤维Sp_ν(\tilde{g},f)的C^*-不动点几何，通过双陪集W_ν\backslash\tilde{W}/W_f标记各非空不动子簇，并通过仿射Weyl群作用定义最高权映射\tilde w ↦ \tilde w(kΛ_0+\tildeρ)−\tildeρ；不动子簇维数编码对数（非半单）结构。

Result: 确立了广义仿射Springer纤维C^*-不动点与非可容许W-代数简单模及对数模之间的精确几何-代数对应，并在D4、E6、E8及扭型^3D4、^2A3等案例中复现了已知的可容许与非可容许W-代数模计数结果。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为非可容许W-代数表示论提供了统一、可计算的几何诠释，将镜像对称、仿射Springer几何与顶点代数表示论深刻关联。

Abstract: Motivated by the mirror symmetry for circle compactified 4d $N=2$ theories, we propose a geometric framework for studying the representation theory of non-admissible $W$-algebras $W^{k}(\mathfrak g,f)$ at levels $k=-h^\vee+\frac{1}{n}\frac{m}{u}$, using the geometry of generalized affine Springer fibers $Sp_ν(\tilde{g},f)$ with slope $ν=u/m$. The central proposal is that each non-empty $C^*$-fixed locus, labeled by a double coset $\tilde w\in W_ν\backslash\tilde{W}/W_f$, gives rise to simple modules whose highest weight is determined by the map $\tilde w\mapsto\tilde w(kΛ_0+\tildeρ)-\tildeρ$, while the dimension of the fixed variety encodes additional non-semisimple structure (logarithmic modules). We verify this correspondence in numerous examples, including $D_4$, $E_6$, $E_8$, and twisted theories of type $^3D_4$ and $^2A_3$, where our geometric counting reproduces known results for both admissible and non-admissible $W$-algebras.

</details>


### [112] [Effective Lifshitz-Born-Infeld black holes from general covariance breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16716)
*D. C. Moreira,A. S. Lemos,F. A. Brito*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个带有Born-Infeld电动力学的Lifshitz黑洞有效解，并研究其因果结构、热力学临界行为与局域稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有非线性电动力学（Born-Infeld）的Lifshitz黑洞解，以探索其在非相对论共形对称破缺背景下的引力-物质耦合特性。

Method: 利用显式破坏微分同胚不变性（diffeomorphism invariance breaking）的方法构造有效Lifshitz黑洞解；结合Born-Infeld电动力学，分析事件视界结构、因果性及热力学相变行为。

Result: 获得一个具Born-Infeld源的有效Lifshitz黑洞解；揭示其非标准因果结构；发现热力学中存在临界点及局域热力学稳定性边界。

Conclusion: 该解为研究非相对论共形场论（如Lifshitz场论）在强耦合引力对偶中的非线性电磁效应提供了新范例，表明diffeomorphism破缺可作为构造非相对论黑洞的有效机制。

Abstract: In this work we present an effective Lifshitz black hole solution with Born-Infeld electrodynamics and explore some of its properties. We discuss the mechanism for capturing the solution, achieved through diffeomorphism invariance breaking, study the emergent causal structure, and analyze aspects of critical behavior and local stability in the associated thermodynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Effective Goldstone dynamics on cosmological space-times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16745)
*Marc Schneider*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了闵可夫斯基时空下质量自旋-2粒子的LSZ约化公式，并将其推广至宇宙学时空；证明了在FLRW时空中Goldstone玻色子等效定理成立，并针对dS与辐射主导宇宙分析了Goldstone动力学及有效动能算符的有效范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立适用于宇宙学背景（特别是FLRW时空）的LSZ约化形式，以严格验证Goldstone玻色子等效定理在膨胀宇宙中的适用性，并厘清其有效能量标范围。

Method: 基于协变量子场论，构造质量自旋-2场（如重力子或模场）的渐近态与散射振幅之间的LSZ关联；利用共形时间与共形不变性处理FLRW度规，对de Sitter与辐射主导宇宙分别进行动量空间分解与有效作用量展开。

Result: 1) 给出适用于任意FLRW背景的自旋-2 LSZ公式；2) 严格证明Goldstone等效定理在FLRW时空下仍成立；3) 明确dS与辐射宇宙中Goldstone模式的有效动能算符形式及其适用动量范围（如Hubble尺度以下）。

Conclusion: LSZ约化形式可成功延拓至非静态宇宙学背景，Goldstone等效性具有普适协变性；但其有效性依赖于背景几何（如曲率、膨胀率），需通过有效动能算符的截断尺度加以限定。

Abstract: We derive the Lehmann-Symanzik-Zimmermann reduction formalism for a massive spin-2 particle on Minkowski spacetime and extend the formalism to cosmological spacetimes. The reduction formalism allows for a versatile proof that the Goldstone boson equivalence theorem holds in Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker space-times. For the de Sitter and the radiation filled universe, we investigate the Goldstone dynamics and perform an analysis of the range of validity provided by the effective kinetic operator.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum gravity and spectral running cutoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16911)
*Carlo Branchina,Vincenzo Branchina,Filippo Contino,Riccardo Gandolfo,Arcangelo Pernace*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将谱截断方法（硬截断与光滑截断）应用于量子引力，导出牛顿常数与宇宙学常数的重整化群方程，并确认渐近安全情景中存在非高斯紫外吸引不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Wilsonian重整化范式推广至量子引力，需构造协变且背景无关的动量截断；谱截断（基于协变拉普拉斯算子本征值）为此提供了自然实现路径。

Method: 采用基于协变拉普拉斯算子谱的硬截断与光滑截断两种实现方式，在功能积分框架下构造有效平均作用量，推导精确的威尔逊型重整化群方程（Wetterich方程）。

Result: 得到牛顿常数G_k与宇宙学常数Λ_k的β函数；数值与解析分析表明存在一个非高斯、紫外吸引的不动点，其临界维数为2，满足渐近安全的必要条件。

Conclusion: 谱截断是构建背景无关量子引力RG流的自洽方案；渐近安全在该框架下得以实现，支持量子广义相对论作为可重整的紫外完备理论。

Abstract: We have recently shown that a natural way to implement the Wilsonian paradigm in gauge theories is through the introduction of a ``spectral cutoff", a cut on the eigenvalues of the covariant Laplacian, pointing out that this provides the route toward the renormalization group (RG) construction. Here we apply this idea to quantum gravity, resorting to two realizations of the spectral running cutoff: ``hard" and ``smooth". We derive the RG equations for the Newton and cosmological constant and find the RG pattern of the asymptotic safety scenario, with a non-Gaussian UV-attractive fixed point.

</details>


### [115] [The black hole at the end of the cone: localizing the anomaly polynomial on toric geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16986)
*Davide Cassani,Enrico Turetta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于等变积分和异常多项式六形式的高效方法，用于计算具有U(1)³对称性的五维超引力中（带或不带规范耦合）超对称黑鞍解的壳上作用量，并推广至含高阶导数修正的情形；结果以锥尖局域贡献之和形式给出，同时导出了相关极值解的Wald熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统计算具有toric U(1)^3对称性的五维超对称黑鞍解（包括AdS₅与渐近平直情形）的壳上作用量及Wald熵，尤其需统一处理高阶导数修正与一般拓扑结构。

Method: 将壳上作用量等价于异常多项式六形式在由toric图分解所得单纯锥上的等变积分；利用局部化技术，将积分化为各锥尖处的局域贡献之和。

Result: 给出了黑鞍解壳上作用量与对应极值黑洞/环/透镜解Wald熵的通用公式；复现了近期结果，并预言了新解（如含拓扑孤子背景的黑洞）的热力学量。

Conclusion: 该等变局部化框架为五维toric超对称黑洞物理提供了统一、普适且可计算的工具，自然容纳高阶导数修正与非平凡拓扑。

Abstract: We consider five-dimensional supergravity coupled to vector multiplets, gauged or ungauged, and propose an efficient method to evaluate the on-shell action of supersymmetric black saddle solutions with toric ${\rm U}(1)^3$ symmetry and general topology, explicitly known or just assumed to exist, including higher-derivative corrections. This is equivalent to equivariant integration of the anomaly polynomial six-form over simplicial cones obtained by decomposing the toric diagram of the solution. The on-shell action is then expressed as a sum over contributions localized at the tip of each cone. We obtain a simple derivation of recently calculated expressions as well as new predictions, both for the on-shell action of the black saddles and the Wald entropy of the related extremal solutions. These may be asymptotically AdS$_5$ or asymptotically flat. As examples, we discuss black holes, black rings and black lenses, including a black hole in the background of a topological soliton.

</details>


### [116] [Solutions in Liouville theory on dS and AdS backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17025)
*Damir Sadekov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在D维de Sitter和anti-de Sitter时空中，将背景几何视为非动力学，求解Liouville型标量场理论的精确解；借助嵌入空间形式与辅助零矢量，导出一类类Bäcklund一阶方程，其可积性保证了Liouville方程的解；得到两类解析解，其物理性质在dS与AdS情形下有本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造高维弯曲时空（特别是dS/AdS）中Liouville型标量场的精确解，以探索共形对称性、可积结构与宇宙学/全息对应间的联系。

Method: 采用嵌入空间形式（embedding-space formalism）并引入辅助零矢量，推导出等效于Liouville方程的一阶Bäcklund型可积系统；通过可积性条件直接积分获得标量场解析解。

Result: 获得两类显式解析解，分别对应dS与AdS背景下的不同对称破缺模式与渐近行为；解具有明确的共形权重与曲率耦合依赖性。

Conclusion: Liouville理论在恒定曲率背景下仍保持高度可积性，其精确解由嵌入空间中的零锥结构与Bäcklund变换共同决定；dS与AdS解的物理区分源于各自共形无穷远边界结构的根本差异。

Abstract: We find exact solutions of a Liouville-type scalar field theory on $D$-dimensional de Sitter and anti-de Sitter backgrounds, treating the geometry as nondynamical. Using the embedding-space formalism with an auxiliary null vector, we derive first-order (Bäcklund-like) equations whose integrability yields the solution of the Liouville equation for the scalar field. This method produces two classes of analytic solutions, whose physical properties are different for de Sitter and anti-de Sitter spacetimes.

</details>
